.

Theorem 7.7
.

Let
\mathchar 28672
be a right-angled Coxeter graph with no vertices of degree zero. Then
\mathchar 28993\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28672\delimiter 84267779\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28724\delimiter 84267779\mathchar 12349\ker\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28993\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28672\delimiter 84267779\mathchar 12833\mathchar 29015\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28672\delimiter 84267779\delimiter 68408078\mathchar 29015\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28672\delimiter 84267779^{\mathchar 560}\delimiter 84054785
, where
\mathchar 29015\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28672\delimiter 84267779
is the corresponding right-angled Coxeter group.

Proof.

Let
\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 24891\ldots\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 29038}
be Artin generators of right-angled Artin group
\mathchar 28993\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28672\delimiter 84267779
. Let
\mathchar 29041\mathchar 12346\mathchar 29015\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28672\delimiter 84267779\mathchar 12833\mathbb{\mathchar 29018}_{\mathchar 28722}^{\mathchar 29038}
the abelianisation map of right-angled Coxeter group
\mathchar 29015\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28672\delimiter 84267779
and
\mathchar 28953\mathchar 12346\mathchar 28993\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28672\delimiter 84267779\mathchar 12833\mathchar 29015\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28672\delimiter 84267779
be the natural quotient map. Let
\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 24891\ldots\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29038}
be generators for
\mathbb{\mathchar 29018}_{\mathchar 28722}^{\mathchar 29038}
, where
\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29041\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28953\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 29033}\delimiter 84054785\delimiter 84054785
. Define a map
\mathchar 28960\mathchar 12346\mathbb{\mathchar 29018}_{\mathchar 28722}^{\mathchar 29038}\mathchar 12833\mathrm{\mathchar 28999\mathchar 29004}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29038\mathchar 24891\mathbb{\mathchar 29018}_{\mathchar 28724}\delimiter 84054785
, given by
\mathchar 28960\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033}\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 12349\tilde{\mathchar 28955}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29025_{\mathchar 29033}\delimiter 84054785\mod\mathchar 28724
. Equation (
7.6
) implies that the matrix
\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29028_{\mathchar 29033\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29034}\delimiter 84054785
is identity modulo 4. Thus, the map
\mathchar 28960
is a group homomorphism. We claim that
\mathchar 28960
is injective. Let
\mathchar 29047\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29034_{\mathchar 28721}}\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29034_{\mathchar 28722}}\mathinner{\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}}\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29034_{\mathchar 29042}}\mathchar 12850\mathbb{\mathchar 29018}_{\mathchar 28722}^{\mathchar 29038}
be a word of length
\mathchar 29042\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28721
, where
\mathchar 29034_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 12604\mathchar 29034_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 12604\mathinner{\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}}\mathchar 12604\mathchar 29034_{\mathchar 29042}
. Using (
7.3
) and induction on
\mathchar 29042
, one can see that if
\mathchar 352\mathchar 12606\mathchar 29034_{\mathchar 29042}
, then the
\mathchar 352
-th row of
\mathchar 28960\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29047\delimiter 84054785
has
\mathchar 28721
in the
\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 352\mathchar 24891\mathchar 352\delimiter 84054785
-entry and
\mathchar 28720
in all other entries. Now, suppose that
\mathchar 29045\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 28721}}\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 28722}}\mathinner{\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}}\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035}}\mathchar 12850\ker\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28960\delimiter 84054785
for some
\mathchar 29035\mathchar 12821\mathchar 28721
. Since
\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033}\mathrel{{\mathchar 566\mathchar 562}}\ker\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28960\delimiter 84054785
for all
\mathchar 29033
, we have
\mathchar 29035\mathchar 12606\mathchar 28721
and we can assume without loss of generality that
\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 12604\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 12604\mathinner{\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}}\mathchar 12604\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035}
Note that the
\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035}
-th row of
\mathchar 28960\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 28721}}\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 28722}}\mathinner{\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}\mathpunct{\mathchar 513}}\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28721}}\delimiter 84054785
has
\mathchar 28721
in the
\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035}\delimiter 84054785
-entry and all other entries are 0. Under our assumption on the graph
\mathchar 28672
, we have that
\mathchar 28960\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035}}\delimiter 84054785
is not equal to the identity matrix, i.e., there exists some
\mathchar 29034
such that the
\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29034\delimiter 84054785
-entry of
\mathchar 28960\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035}}\delimiter 84054785
is either
\mathchar 28722
or
\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28722
,
\mathchar 29034
-th column of
\mathchar 28960\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29045_{\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035}}\delimiter 84054785
has
\mathchar 28721
in the
\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29034\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29034\delimiter 84054785
-entry, and the
\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29034\delimiter 84054785
-entry is
\mathchar 28722
or
\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28722
, while all other entries are 0. Thus,
\mathchar 28960\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29045\delimiter 84054785
has a non-zero
\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29033_{\mathchar 29035}\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29034\delimiter 84054785
-entry equal to
\mathchar 28722
or
\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28722
, which is a contradiction. Hence,
\mathchar 28960
is injective, and we obtain
\mathchar 28993\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28672\delimiter 84267779\delimiter 67482370\mathchar 28724\delimiter 84267779\mathchar 12349\ker\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29041\mathchar 8718\mathchar 28953\delimiter 84054785
. This completes the proof of the theorem.
∎
